L1614: Figure 6 is a flowchart illustrating the overview of the DIA3002 Study Design, which consists of a Pretreatment Phase and a Double-Blind Treatment Phase.
Pretreatment Phase:
Subjects with T2DM 18–80 years on protocol-specified doses* of metformin and SU with HbA_{1c} \ge 7\% and \le 10.5\%: Direct to Week -2 visit to start single-blind run-in.
Subjects with T2DM 18–80 years on combination of metformin+SU, below protocol-specified* doses, with HbA_{1c} \ge 7.5\%: Up-titrate MF / SU (up to 4 weeks) to achieve Stable MF/SU doses (8 weeks).
Double-Blind Treatment Phase:
The phase is divided into the Core Double-Blind Period and the Double-Blind Extension Period.
Run-in Period (Week -2 to Day 1 Baseline):
Subjects enter a Single-blind placebo run-in.
Subjects must meet criteria: HbA_{1c} \ge 7\% and \le 10.5\%, and Site FS glucose \ge 110 and <270 mg/dL.
Subjects continue stable doses of metformin / SU.
Randomization (R) at Day 1 Baseline:
Placebo arm.
Canagliflozin 100 mg arm.
Canagliflozin 300 mg arm.
Core Double-Blind Period (Week 26): End Core Double-blind Period / Start Extension Double-blind Period.
Double-Blind Extension Period (Week 52): End Double-Blind Phase.
Timeline Events:
Optional Pre-screening Visit
Screening Visit
AHA Adjustment Period Start
Week -2 Single-blind Placebo Run-in Start
Day 1 Baseline
Week 26 End Core Double-blind Period / Start Extension Double-blind Period
Week 52 End Double-Blind Phase
Protocol-Specified Dose: metformin \ge 2,000 mg/day (or \ge 1,500 mg/day, if intolerant of higher dose); SU doses provided in Attachment 2.
Abbreviations: AHA=antihyperglycemic agent; FS=fingerstick; MF=metformin; R=randomization; SU=sulfonylurea.
L1615: Flowchart illustrating the DIA3015 Study Design, divided into a Pretreatment Phase and a Double-Blind Treatment Phase.
Pretreatment Phase:
Subjects with T2DM \ge18 years of age on protocol-specified doses* of metformin and SU with HbA1c \ge7.0% and \le10.5%: Direct to Week -2 visit to start single-blind run-in.
Subjects with T2DM \ge18 years of age on combination of metformin+SU, at less than protocol-specified* doses, with HbA1c \ge7.5%: Enter the AHA Adjustment Period*.
AHA Adjustment Period*:
Up-titrate metformin/SU (up to 4 weeks).
Stable metformin/SU doses (8 weeks).
Subjects meeting the criteria for the single-blind placebo run-in (HbA1c \ge7.0% and \le10.5%) proceed to the Single-blind placebo run-in.
Single-blind placebo run-in: Leads to Randomization (R).
Randomization (R): Subjects are randomized to either Canagliflozin 300 mg or Sitagliptin 100 mg.
Double-Blind Treatment Phase:
Both treatment arms receive Canagliflozin 300 mg or Sitagliptin 100 mg, respectively.
Continue stable doses of metformin / SU** throughout the treatment phase.
Timeline: Optional Pre-screening Visit, Screening Visit, AHA Adjustment Period Start, Week -2 Single-Blind Placebo Run-in Start, Day 1 Baseline, Week 52 End Double-Blind Phase.
Legend:
*protocol-specified = metformin \ge2,000 mg/day (1,500 mg/day for subjects intolerant of higher doses); sulphonylurea doses are defined in Attachment 2
AHA=antihyperglycemic agent; FS=fingerstick; R=randomization; SU=sulphonylurea
L1616: Flowchart illustrating the DIA3012 Study Design, divided into three phases: Pretreatment Phase, Core Double-blind Treatment Period, and Extension Double-blind Treatment Period.
Pretreatment Phase (Screening Visit to Week -2 Single-Blind Placebo Run-in Start):
Eligibility criteria include T2DM, 18-80 yrs, on MET and PIO at protocol-specified doses*, HbA_{1c} \ge 7\% and \le 10.5\%.
Eligibility criteria include T2DM, 18-80 yrs, on MET and PIO at < protocol-specified doses*, HbA_{1c} \ge 7.5\% or \le 10.5\%.
Eligibility criteria include On MET and ROSI, HbA_{1c} \ge 7\% and \le 10.5\%.
Eligibility criteria include On PPAR\gamma (PIO or ROSI) + another AHA, HbA_{1c} \ge 7\% and \le 10.5\%.
Eligibility criteria include On MET and PPAR\gamma (PIO or ROSI) and SU or DPP-4 inhibitor, HbA_{1c} \ge 6.5\% and \le 9.5\%.
Subjects proceed to the Week -2 Single-Blind Placebo Run-in Start visit. During this period, titration is needed to get to protocol-specified* doses of MET and PIO / Stop other AHAs (Up to 4 weeks). Subjects must maintain stable, protocol-specified* doses of metformin and pioglitazone (8 weeks).
Core Double-blind Treatment Period (Week -2 Single-Blind Placebo Run-in Start to Week 26 End Core Double-blind period / Start Double-blind Extension):
Subjects are randomized (R) based on Site FS glucose \ge 110 to <270 mg/dL.
Randomization leads to three treatment arms:
Canagliflozin 300 mg
Canagliflozin 100 mg
Placebo
Extension Double-blind Treatment Period (Week 26 End Core Double-blind period / Start Double-blind Extension to Week 52 End Double-blind Phase):
Subjects on Placebo are crossed over to Sitagliptin 100 mg.

L1618: Diagram illustrating the Overview of DIA3008 Study Design (Cohort A).
Eligibility Criteria: Patients with T2DM, HbA_{1c} 7-10.5%, not on antihyperglycemic agent (AHA) or on any AHA(s) in a stable (\ge 8 weeks) regimen, either:
\ge 30 yrs of age, with history of documented CV disease (\ge 70\%) or
\ge 50 yrs of age with 2 or more CV risk factors (< 30\%)
Run-in Period: Single-blind placebo run-in and diet/exercise stable period (Week -3 Screening to Week -2 Run-in Start). During this period, subjects received Diet and exercise counseling and Counseling on hypoglycemia recognition and management. Stable AHA doses were maintained. If on SFU or insulin, fasting fingerstick glucose at site > 110 mg/dL.
Randomization (R): Occurs on Day 1 Baseline.
Double-Blind Treatment Phase: The phase is divided into two periods:
AHA regimen stable period: Subjects received Canagliflozin 300 mg or Canagliflozin 100 mg.
Adjust AHA regimen to achieve glycemic targets: Subjects received Placebo.
The treatment phase runs from Day 1 Baseline to Week 18. An Interim analysis was conducted to determine Cohort B sample size (and determine recruitment of Cohort B) prior to the Final Study Visit.
L1619: Figure 11: LS Mean Change in HbA1c From Baseline Over Time in DIA3005 (Main Study) - LOCF. The plot shows the LS Mean Change in HbA1c (%) from Baseline over time (Baseline, Week 6 LOCF, Week 12 LOCF, Week 18 LOCF, Week 26 LOCF) for three groups: Placebo, Cana 100 mg, and Cana 300 mg. The Y-axis ranges from -1.1 to 0.3. The Placebo group shows minimal change (around 0.0%). The Cana 100 mg group shows a significant decrease, reaching approximately -0.7% by Week 6 LOCF and stabilizing around -0.7% to -0.8% thereafter. The Cana 300 mg group shows the greatest reduction, reaching approximately -0.9% by Week 6 LOCF and stabilizing around -0.9% to -1.0% thereafter. Error bars represent standard error (SE).

L1627: Figure 19 is a line graph showing the Mean Change (\pm-SE) in eGFR (\text{mL}/\text{min}/1.73\text{m}^2) from Baseline Over Time (Weeks 0, 6, 12, 18, and 26) for the DS1 Dataset Safety Analysis Set. The Y-axis represents the Mean Change in eGFR, ranging from -6 to 0. The X-axis represents time points. Three treatment groups are compared: Placebo, Canagliflozin 100 mg, and Canagliflozin 300 mg. The graph shows that all groups start at a mean change of 0 at Baseline. By Week 6, the Placebo group shows a slight decrease (around -0.5 \text{mL}/\text{min}/1.73\text{m}^2), while the Canagliflozin groups show a significant decrease (around -4.5 to -5.0 \text{mL}/\text{min}/1.73\text{m}^2). By Week 26, the Placebo group returns to near baseline (around -0.5 \text{mL}/\text{min}/1.73\text{m}^2), while the Canagliflozin groups show a mean change around -3.0 to -3.5 \text{mL}/\text{min}/1.73\text{m}^2. The Canagliflozin 300 mg group consistently shows a greater decrease in eGFR compared to the 100 mg group across all time points.

L1628: Line graph showing Mean Change (+/-SE) in eGFR (mL/min/1.73m2) from Baseline Over Time (DIA3004: Modified Intent-to-Treat Analysis Set).
The X-axis represents time points: BASELINE, WEEK 3, WEEK 6, WEEK 12, WEEK 18, WEEK 26, and WEEK 26 LOCF. The Y-axis represents eGFR (mL/min/1.73m2), ranging from -7 to 1.
Legend:
Planned Treatment: Placebo (open circles), Cana 100 mg (stars), Cana 300 mg (solid circles).
The graph illustrates that all treatment groups show a significant decline in eGFR from Baseline (0 change) at Week 3. The decline is dose-dependent, with Cana 300 mg showing the largest decline (reaching approximately -6 mL/min/1.73m2 at Week 3). The decline appears to stabilize or slightly increase over time, particularly for the Cana 300 mg group, which reaches approximately -4 mL/min/1.73m2 at Week 26 LOCF.
